We’ve tried many of the nicer restaurants in the area and, while many have great atmosphere, few seem to have the kind of food and prices that would bring me back. Bolo does not have that problem. I love everything about this place. It is a little off the beaten track but not inconvenient. While other places have a 1-2 hour wait, Bolo you can walk right in and have a seat at your table. The atmosphere is lovely. The driftwood fish sculptures, intimate lighting and Southern architectural style of the restaurant is just perfect. So far, the wait staff has been very pleasant and informative on their food. Prices are very attractive for what you get. I simply can’t get past the 8oz Sirloin that looks and tastes much more like a high end fillet! Crunchy, well seasoned exterior and soft, tender, pink deliciousness on the inside - again, more like a fillet. I’m guessing aged beef because it is effortless to eat. Their crispy Brussels sprouts are quite tasty with just a little grind of sea salt from their table-side salt grinder. A nice selection of wine, beer and cocktails top things off nicely. I honestly can’t figure why they aren’t jam packed.",

--- entity_google_reviews [row 4 of 5] ---
review_id: 2979
entity_slug: bolo-steak-seafood
google_review_name: places/ChIJR3jFMAAHmogRiQMZkYLPpSM/reviews/Ci9DQUlRQUNvZENodHljRjlvT2pNelREazBjRnBGY0VKYVRuQXhSblV0WXpab1dIYxAB
rating: 5
review_text: This is my second trip within a week to Bolo! In town at Orange Beach with family and always trying to find a place that is a good value. We’ve tried many of the nicer restaurants in the area and, while many have great atmosphere, few seem to have the kind of food and prices that would bring me back. Bolo does not have that problem. I love everything about this place. It is a little off the beaten track but not inconvenient. While other places have a 1-2 hour wait, Bolo you can walk right in and have a seat at your table. The atmosphere is lovely. The driftwood fish sculptures, intimate lighting and Southern architectural style of the restaurant is just perfect. So far, the wait staff has been very pleasant and informative on their food. Prices are very attractive for what you get. I simply can’t get past the 8oz Sirloin that looks and tastes much more like a high end fillet! Crunchy, well seasoned exterior and soft, tender, pink deliciousness on the inside - again, more like a fillet. I’m guessing aged beef because it is effortless to eat. Their crispy Brussels sprouts are quite tasty with just a little grind of sea salt from their table-side salt grinder. A nice selection of wine, beer and cocktails top things off nicely. I honestly can’t figure why they aren’t jam packed.
language_code: en
author_name: Kelly Cunningham
author_profile_url: https://www.google.com/maps/contrib/111054206100958611653/reviews

--- entity_google_reviews [row 5 of 5] ---
review_id: 2980
entity_slug: bolo-steak-seafood
google_review_name: places/ChIJR3jFMAAHmogRiQMZkYLPpSM/reviews/ChZDSUhNMG9nS0VJQ0FnSURYbHNhYVVBEAE
rating: 5
review_text: We 3 ladies decided on the recommendation of a friend to try this restaurant. Walking in the atmosphere was serene and a treat for the eyes.. soft lighting with plenty of full length windows and the art was a treat for the eyes.. Gabe was our knowledgeable and upbeat server. He answered all our questions about drinks and food. When our drinks arrived we were all pleased, my red wine, chosen from a lone list of choices was excellent and a very nice pour! The brown sugar old fashioned was perfect .. no draft beer but plenty of selection for craft and domestic beers was available. Then our meals came.. wow!!  The special; rainbow trout with spaetzl and mushrooms, prime rib poorboy and fries and filet with crispy Brussels spouts .. We ate every bite!! Finally we ordered the in house made triple chocolate torte and shared it 🤤😋we all said it was one of the best desserts ever!!!
So go!! They have a bar that anybody would love to go to watch the game and 3 separate dining areas plus plenty of parking!

--- entity_reviews [row 4 of 5] ---
id: 764fa6cb-601e-45b9-befa-12be06da1d8d
entity_slug: bolo-steak-seafood
reviewer_name: Kelly Cunningham
rating: 5
body: This is my second trip within a week to Bolo! In town at Orange Beach with family and always trying to find a place that is a good value. We’ve tried many of the nicer restaurants in the area and, while many have great atmosphere, few seem to have the kind of food and prices that would bring me back. Bolo does not have that problem. I love everything about this place. It is a little off the beaten track but not inconvenient. While other places have a 1-2 hour wait, Bolo you can walk right in and have a seat at your table. The atmosphere is lovely. The driftwood fish sculptures, intimate lighting and Southern architectural style of the restaurant is just perfect. So far, the wait staff has been very pleasant and informative on their food. Prices are very attractive for what you get. I simply can’t get past the 8oz Sirloin that looks and tastes much more like a high end fillet! Crunchy, well seasoned exterior and soft, tender, pink deliciousness on the inside - again, more like a fillet. I’m guessing aged beef because it is effortless to eat. Their crispy Brussels sprouts are quite tasty with just a little grind of sea salt from their table-side salt grinder. A nice selection of wine, beer and cocktails top things off nicely. I honestly can’t figure why they aren’t jam packed.
verified_purchase: false
helpful_count: 0
approved: true
created_at: 2025-10-15T01:33:19.751708+00:00

--- entity_reviews [row 5 of 5] ---
id: b620f03c-fafe-4cfa-a34e-c0575d6411d2
entity_slug: bolo-steak-seafood
reviewer_name: Cherie Allred
rating: 5
body: We 3 ladies decided on the recommendation of a friend to try this restaurant. Walking in the atmosphere was serene and a treat for the eyes.. soft lighting with plenty of full length windows and the art was a treat for the eyes.. Gabe was our knowledgeable and upbeat server. He answered all our questions about drinks and food. When our drinks arrived we were all pleased, my red wine, chosen from a lone list of choices was excellent and a very nice pour! The brown sugar old fashioned was perfect .. no draft beer but plenty of selection for craft and domestic beers was available. Then our meals came.. wow!!  The special; rainbow trout with spaetzl and mushrooms, prime rib poorboy and fries and filet with crispy Brussels spouts .. We ate every bite!! Finally we ordered the in house made triple chocolate torte and shared it 🤤😋we all said it was one of the best desserts ever!!!
So go!! They have a bar that anybody would love to go to watch the game and 3 separate dining areas plus plenty of parking!
